13. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;

15. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
17. 
18. // The Blank Application template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402347&clcid=0x409
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     /// <summary>
23.     /// Provides application-specific behavior to supplement the default Application class.
24.     /// </summary>
25.     sealed partial class App : Application
26.     {
27.         /// <summary>
28.         /// Initializes the singleton application object.  This is the first line of authored code
29.         /// executed, and as such is the logical equivalent of main() or WinMain().
30.         /// </summary>
31.         public App()
32.         {
33.             this.InitializeComponent();
34.             this.Construct();
35.         }
36. 
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Invoked when the application is launched normally by the end user.  Other entry points
39.         /// will be used such as when the application is launched to open a specific file.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         /// <param name="e">Details about the launch request and process.</param>
42.         protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e)
43.         {
44. 
45. #if DEBUG
46.             if (System.Diagnostics.Debugger.IsAttached)
47.             {
48.                 this.DebugSettings.EnableFrameRateCounter = false;
49.             }
50. #endif
51. 
52.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
53. 
54.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
55.             // just ensure that the window is active
56.             if (rootFrame == null)
57.             {
58.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
59.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
60.                 // Set the default language
61.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
62. 
63.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
64. 
65.                 if (e.PreviousExecutionState == ApplicationExecutionState.Terminated)
66.                 {
67.                     //TODO: Load state from previously suspended application
68.                 }
69. 
70.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
71.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
72.             }
73. 
74.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
75.             {
76.                 // When the navigation stack isn't restored navigate to the first page,
77.                 // configuring the new page by passing required information as a navigation
78.                 // parameter
79.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), e.Arguments);
80.             }
81. 
82.             // Ensure the current window is active
83.             Window.Current.Activate();
84. 
85.             // Some samples want access to the LaunchActivatedEventArgs.
86.             LaunchCompleted(e);
87.         }
88. 
89.         private Frame CreateRootFrame()
90.         {
91.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
92. 
93.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
94.             // just ensure that the window is active
95.             if (rootFrame == null)
96.             {
97.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
98.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
99. 
100.                 // Set the default language
101.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
102.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
103. 
104.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
105.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
106.             }
107. 
108.             return rootFrame;
109.         }
110. 
111.         /// <summary>
112.         /// Invoked when Navigation to a certain page fails
113.         /// </summary>
114.         /// <param name="sender">The Frame which failed navigation</param>
115.         /// <param name="e">Details about the navigation failure</param>
116.         void OnNavigationFailed(object sender, NavigationFailedEventArgs e)
117.         {
118.             throw new Exception("Failed to load Page " + e.SourcePageType.FullName);
119.         }
120. 
121.         // Add any application constructor code in here.
122.         partial void Construct();
123. 
124.         // Add any OnLaunched customization here.
125.         partial void LaunchCompleted(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e);
126.     }
127. }

43. namespace SDKTemplate
44. {
45.     class BackgroundTaskSample
46.     {
47.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskEntryPoint = "Tasks.SampleBackgroundTask";
48.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskName = "SampleBackgroundTask";
49.         public static string SampleBackgroundTaskProgress = "";
50.         public static bool SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = false;
51. 
52.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName = "SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition";
53.         public static string SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionProgress = "";
54.         public static bool SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = false;
55. 
56.         public const string ServicingCompleteTaskEntryPoint = "Tasks.ServicingComplete";
57.         public const string ServicingCompleteTaskName = "ServicingCompleteTask";
58.         public static string ServicingCompleteTaskProgress = "";
59.         public static bool ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = false;
60. 
61.         public const string TimeTriggeredTaskName = "TimeTriggeredTask";
62.         public static string TimeTriggeredTaskProgress = "";
63.         public static bool TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = false;
64. 
65.         public const string ApplicationTriggerTaskName = "ApplicationTriggerTask";
66.         public static string ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress = "";
67.         public static string ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "";
68.         public static bool ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = false;
69. 
70.         public const string GroupedBackgroundTaskName = "GroupedBackgroundTask";
71.         public const string BackgroundTaskGroupId = "3F2504E0-5F89-41D3-9A0C-0405E82C3333";
72.         public const string BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName = "Background Task Group";
73.         public static string GroupedBackgroundTaskProgress = "";
74.         public static bool GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = false;
75. 
76.         /// <summary>
77.         /// Register a background task with the specified taskEntryPoint, name, trigger,
78.         /// and condition (optional).
79.         /// </summary>
80.         /// <param name="taskEntryPoint">Task entry point for the background task.</param>
81.         /// <param name="name">A name for the background task.</param>
82.         /// <param name="trigger">The trigger for the background task.</param>
83.         /// <param name="condition">An optional conditional event that must be true for the task to fire.</param>
84.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistration RegisterBackgroundTask(String taskEntryPoint, String name, IBackgroundTrigger trigger, IBackgroundCondition condition, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
85.         {
86.             if (TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(name))
87.             {
88.                 // If the user denies access, the task will not run.
89.                 var requestTask = BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync();
90.             }
91. 
92.             var builder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
93. 
94.             builder.Name = name;
95.             builder.TaskEntryPoint = taskEntryPoint;
96.             builder.SetTrigger(trigger);
97. 
98.             if (condition != null)
99.             {
100.                 builder.AddCondition(condition);
101. 
102.                 //
103.                 // If the condition changes while the background task is executing then it will
104.                 // be canceled.
105.                 //
106.                 builder.CancelOnConditionLoss = true;
107.             }
108. 
109.             if (group != null)
110.             {
111.                 builder.TaskGroup = group;
112.             }
113. 
114.             BackgroundTaskRegistration task = builder.Register();
115. 
116.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, true);
117. 
118.             //
119.             // Remove previous completion status.
120.             //
121.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
122.             settings.Values.Remove(name);
123. 
124.             return task;
125.         }
126. 
127.         /// <summary>
128.         /// Unregister background tasks with specified name.
129.         /// </summary>
130.         /// <param name="name">Name of the background task to unregister.</param>
131.         public static void UnregisterBackgroundTasks(String name, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
132.         {
133.             //
134.             // If the given task group is registered then loop through all background tasks associated with it
135.             // and unregister any with the given name.
136.             //
137.             if (group != null)
138.             {
139.                 foreach (var cur in group.AllTasks)
140.                 {
141.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
142.                     {
143.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
144.                     }
145.                 }
146.             }
147.             else
148.             {
149.                 //
150.                 // Loop through all ungrouped background tasks and unregister any with the given name.
151.                 //
152.                 foreach (var cur in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
153.                 {
154.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
155.                     {
156.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
157.                     }
158.                 }
159.             }
160. 
161.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, false);
162.         }
163. 
164.         /// <summary>
165.         /// Retrieve a registered background task group. If no group is registered with the given id,
166.         /// then create a new one and return it.
167.         /// </summary>
168.         /// <returns>The task group associated with the given id</returns>
169.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup GetTaskGroup(string id, string groupName)
170.         {
171.             var group = BackgroundTaskRegistration.GetTaskGroup(id);
172. 
173.             if (group == null)
174.             {
175.                 group = new BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup(id, groupName);
176.             }
177. 
178.             return group;
179.         }
180. 
181.         /// <summary>
182.         /// Store the registration status of a background task with a given name.
183.         /// </summary>
184.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to store registration status for.</param>
185.         /// <param name="registered">TRUE if registered, FALSE if unregistered.</param>
186.         public static void UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(String name, bool registered)
187.         {
188.             switch (name)
189.             {
190.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
191.                     SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
192.                     break;
193.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
194.                     SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = registered;
195.                     break;
196.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
197.                     ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = registered;
198.                     break;
199.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
200.                     TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = registered;
201.                     break;
202.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
203.                     ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = registered;
204.                     break;
205.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
206.                     GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
207.                     break;
208.             }
209.         }
210. 
211.         /// <summary>
212.         /// Get the registration / completion status of the background task with
213.         /// given name.
214.         /// </summary>
215.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to retreive registration status.</param>
216.         public static String GetBackgroundTaskStatus(String name)
217.         {
218.             var registered = false;
219.             switch (name)
220.             {
221.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
222.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
223.                     break;
224.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
225.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered;
226.                     break;
227.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
228.                     registered = ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered;
229.                     break;
230.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
231.                     registered = TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered;
232.                     break;
233.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
234.                     registered = ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
235.                     break;
236.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
237.                     registered = GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered;
238.                     break;
239.             }
240. 
241.             var status = registered ? "Registered" : "Unregistered";
242. 
243.             object taskStatus;
244.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
245.             if (settings.Values.TryGetValue(name, out taskStatus))
246.             {
247.                 status += " - " + taskStatus.ToString();
248.             }
249. 
250.             return status;
251.         }
252. 
253.         /// <summary>
254.         /// Determine if task with given name requires background access.
255.         /// </summary>
256.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to query background access requirement.</param>
257.         public static bool TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(String name)
258.         {
259.             if ((name == TimeTriggeredTaskName) ||
260.                 (name == ApplicationTriggerTaskName))
261.             {
262.                 return true;
263.             }
264.             else
265.             {
266.                 return false;
267.             }
268.         }
269.     }
270. }

46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;

51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53.

55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74.

76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;

92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}

11. using System;
12. using System.Diagnostics;
13. using System.Threading;
14. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
15. using Windows.Foundation;
16. using Windows.Storage;
17. using Windows.System.Threading;
18. 
19. //
20. // The namespace for the background tasks.
21. //
22. namespace Tasks
23. {
24.     //
25.     // A background task always implements the IBackgroundTask interface.
26.     //
27.     public sealed class SampleBackgroundTask : IBackgroundTask
28.     {
29.         BackgroundTaskCancellationReason _cancelReason = BackgroundTaskCancellationReason.Abort;
30.         volatile bool _cancelRequested = false;
31.         BackgroundTaskDeferral _deferral = null;
32.         ThreadPoolTimer _periodicTimer = null;
33.         uint _progress = 0;
34.         IBackgroundTaskInstance _taskInstance = null;
35. 
36.         //
37.         // The Run method is the entry point of a background task.
38.         //
39.         public void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
40.         {
41.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + taskInstance.Task.Name + " Starting...");
42. 
43.             //
44.             // Query BackgroundWorkCost
45.             // Guidance: If BackgroundWorkCost is high, then perform only the minimum amount
46.             // of work in the background task and return immediately.
47.             //
48.             var cost = BackgroundWorkCost.CurrentBackgroundWorkCost;
49.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
50.             settings.Values["BackgroundWorkCost"] = cost.ToString();
51. 
52.             //
53.             // Associate a cancellation handler with the background task.
54.             //
55.             taskInstance.Canceled += new BackgroundTaskCanceledEventHandler(OnCanceled);
56. 
57.             //
58.             // Get the deferral object from the task instance, and take a reference to the taskInstance;
59.             //
60.             _deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
61.             _taskInstance = taskInstance;
62. 
63.             _periodicTimer = ThreadPoolTimer.CreatePeriodicTimer(new TimerElapsedHandler(PeriodicTimerCallback), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
64.         }
65. 
66.         //
67.         // Handles background task cancellation.
68.         //
69.         private void OnCanceled(IBackgroundTaskInstance sender, BackgroundTaskCancellationReason reason)
70.         {
71.             //
72.             // Indicate that the background task is canceled.
73.             //
74.             _cancelRequested = true;
75.             _cancelReason = reason;
76. 
77.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + sender.Task.Name + " Cancel Requested...");
78.         }
79. 
80.         //
81.         // Simulate the background task activity.
82.         //
83.         private void PeriodicTimerCallback(ThreadPoolTimer timer)
84.         {
85.             if ((_cancelRequested == false) && (_progress < 100))
86.             {
87.                 _progress += 10;
88.                 _taskInstance.Progress = _progress;
89.             }
90.             else
91.             {
92.                 _periodicTimer.Cancel();
93. 
94.                 var key = _taskInstance.Task.Name;
95. 
96.                 //
97.                 // Record that this background task ran.
98.                 //
99.                 String taskStatus = (_progress < 100) ? "Canceled with reason: " + _cancelReason.ToString() : "Completed";
100.                 var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
101.                 settings.Values[key] = taskStatus;
102.                 Debug.WriteLine("Background " + _taskInstance.Task.Name + settings.Values[key]);
103. 
104.                 //
105.                 // Indicate that the background task has completed.
106.                 //
107.                 _deferral.Complete();
108.             }
109.         }
110.     }
111. }
112.

Clean up duplicates\nDelete the `BackgroundTask/` subfolder under target (those are duplicates from the UWP project subfolder that got copied in). The root-level files are the ones to keep.\n\n### 2. Fix App.xaml / App.xaml.cs\nThe UWP App.xaml.cs uses `Window.Current`, `Frame`, and `OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs)`. \n\nFor WinUI 3:\n- `App.xaml.cs` needs to use `Microsoft.UI.Xaml` namespace\n- `OnLaunched` takes `Microsoft.UI.Xaml.LaunchActivatedEventArgs`\n- Replace `Window.Current.Content` pattern with `MainWindow`\n- The app should create a `MainWindow`, navigate its frame to `MainPage`\n\nThe App.xaml.cs has TODOs injected. Check them and fix. The pattern for WinUI3 App.xaml.cs is:\n```csharp\npublic sealed partial class App : Application\n{\n    public App() { this.InitializeComponent(); }\n    \n    private MainWindow window;\n    \n    protected override void OnLaunched(Microsoft.UI.Xaml.LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)\n    {\n        window = new MainWindow();\n        window.Activate();\n    }\n}\n```\n\nBUT we need to navigate to MainPage. The MainWindow needs to host a Frame that shows MainPage. The WinUI3 project already has a `MainWindow.xaml` - adapt it to host the UWP-style navigation.\n\n### 3. Fix MainWindow.xaml + MainWindow.xaml.cs\nThe scaffold's MainWindow is a simple window. Adapt it to:\n- Host a Frame in its content\n- On load, navigate to MainPage\n\nReplace the scaffold's MainWindow with:\n```xml\n<Window x:Class=\"SDKTemplate.MainWindow\" ...>\n    <Frame x:Name=\"rootFrame\"/>\n</Window>\n```\nAnd in MainWindow.xaml.cs, navigate the rootFrame to MainPage on Loaded.\n\nActually, simpler approach: just set the Window content directly to a MainPage instance:\n\nMainWindow.xaml:\n```xml\n<Window x:Class=\"SDKTemplate.MainWindow\"\n    xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\"\n    xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\"\n    xmlns:local=\"using:SDKTemplate\"\n    Title=\"Background Tasks\">\n</Window>\n```\n\nMainWindow.xaml.cs:\n```csharp\npublic sealed partial class MainWindow : Window\n{\n    public MainWindow()\n    {\n        this.InitializeComponent();\n        var rootFrame = new Frame();\n        this.Content = rootFrame;\n        rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage));\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Fix MainPage.xaml / MainPage.xaml.cs\nThe UWP MainPage.xaml is a Page with SplitView pattern. The XAML should work largely as-is in WinUI3 (namespace already rewritten from `Windows.UI.Xaml` to `Microsoft.UI.Xaml` by the bootstrap).\n\nMainPage.xaml.cs has TODOs for:\n- `Window.Current.Bounds` → use `AppWindow` sizing\n- `Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, ...)` → use `DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(...)`\n- `Windows.UI.Colors` → `Microsoft.UI` (already rewritten)\n- `CoreDispatcher` → `DispatcherQueue`\n\nPattern for WinUI3 MainPage.xaml.cs:\n```csharp\n// Instead of: await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => ...);\n// Use: DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => ...);\n\n// Instead of: if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)\n// Use: check against a fixed threshold or use ActualWidth\n```\n\nFor the `NotifyUser` method's dispatcher pattern:\n```csharp\npublic void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n{\n    if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n    {\n        UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));\n    }\n}\n```\n\nFor `Window.Current.Bounds`:\n```csharp\n// Just use a fixed value or check ActualWidth\nif (ActualWidth < 640)  // or just remove the check\n```\n\nFor `Windows.UI.Colors.Green` etc.:\nThe bootstrap already rewrote `Windows.UI.` to `Microsoft.UI.`. `Microsoft.UI.Colors` exists. Fix SampleConfiguration.cs\nThis file has:\n- Background task registration code that uses `Windows.ApplicationModel.Background` APIs\n- These are still available in WinUI3! `BackgroundTaskRegistration`, `BackgroundTaskBuilder`, etc. all work\n- `ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings` - works in WinUI3\n- But `BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync()` - check if this is supported\n\nNote: `BackgroundExecutionManager` IS available in WinUI3 (it's a Windows API not a UWP-specific API). However, the behavior may differ for desktop apps. In WinUI3 packaged desktop apps, you don't need to call `RequestAccessAsync` - background tasks work through the app's MSIX manifest extensions.\n\n### 7. Fix Tasks/SampleBackgroundTask.cs and Tasks/ServicingComplete.cs\nThese are IBackgroundTask implementations. They use `Windows.ApplicationModel.Background` and `Windows.Storage` which are still available. The namespace rewrite should handle most of this.\n\nCheck for TODOs in these files.\n\n### 8. Package.appxmanifest\nThe WinUI3 template provides a Package.appxmanifest. The UWP version had background task extensions. For WinUI3, you need to add the background task extensions to the manifest:\n\n```xml\n<Extensions>\n    <Extension Category=\"windows.backgroundTasks\" EntryPoint=\"Tasks.SampleBackgroundTask\">\n        <BackgroundTasks>\n            <Task Type=\"systemEvent\"/>\n            <Task Type=\"timer\"/>\n        </BackgroundTasks>\n    </Extension>\n    <Extension Category=\"windows.backgroundTasks\" EntryPoint=\"Tasks.ServicingComplete\">\n        <BackgroundTasks>\n            <Task Type=\"systemEvent\"/>\n        </BackgroundTasks>\n    </Extension>\n</Extensions>\n```\n\nAlso add the `runFullTrust` capability and `rescap` namespace for WinUI3:\n```xml\n<Package\n  xmlns:rescap=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/appx/manifest/foundation/windows10/restrictedcapabilities\"\n  IgnorableNamespaces=\"uap mp rescap\">\n...\n<Capabilities>\n    <Capability Name=\"internetClient\"/>\n    <rescap:Capability Name=\"runFullTrust\"/>\n</Capabilities>\n```\n\nAnd update `TargetDeviceFamily` for desktop:\n```xml\n<TargetDeviceFamily Name=\"Windows.Desktop\" MinVersion=\"10.0.17763.0\" MaxVersionTested=\"10.0.19041.0\"/>\n```\n\n### 9. Styles/Styles.xaml\nThe UWP Styles may reference `Windows.UI.Xaml` namespace or theme resources. The bootstrap should have rewritten namespaces. Check for any remaining issues.\n\n### 10. App.xaml\nThe App.xaml references `Styles/Styles.xaml`. In WinUI3, this should still work.\n\n## Important Notes\n\n1. **Tasks folder**: The `Tasks/` folder contains background task classes. In WinUI3, background tasks for packaged desktop apps can be in-process (same DLL) or out-of-process. For simplicity, keep them in the main project as a separate namespace `Tasks`.\n\n2. **`BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup`**: This class IS available in WinUI3 (`Windows.ApplicationModel.Background.BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup`).\n\n3. **`ApplicationTrigger`** (Scenario5): This trigger IS supported in WinUI3 desktop apps. 24.     /// </summary>

26.     {
27.         /// <summary>
28.         /// Initializes the singleton application object.  This is the first line of authored code
29.         /// executed, and as such is the logical equivalent of main() or WinMain().
30.         /// </summary>
31.         public App()
32.         {
33.             this.InitializeComponent();
34.             this.Construct();
35.         }
36. 
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Invoked when the application is launched normally by the end user.  Other entry points
39.         /// will be used such as when the application is launched to open a specific file.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         /// <param name="e">Details about the launch request and process.</param>
42.         protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e)
43.         {
44. 
45. #if DEBUG
46.             if (System.Diagnostics.Debugger.IsAttached)
47.             {
48.                 this.DebugSettings.EnableFrameRateCounter = false;
49.             }
50. #endif
51. 
52.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
53.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
54. 
55.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
56.             // just ensure that the window is active
57.             if (rootFrame == null)
58.             {
59.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
60.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
61.                 // Set the default language
62.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
63. 
64.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
65. 
66.                 if (e.PreviousExecutionState == ApplicationExecutionState.Terminated)
67.                 {
68.                     //TODO: Load state from previously suspended application
69.                 }
70. 
71.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
72.                 // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
73.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
74.             }
75. 
76.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
77.             {
78.                 // When the navigation stack isn't restored navigate to the first page,
79.                 // configuring the new page by passing required information as a navigation
80.                 // parameter
81.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), e.Arguments);
82.             }
83. 
84.             // Ensure the current window is active
85.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
86.             Window.Current.Activate();
87. 
88.             // Some samples want access to the LaunchActivatedEventArgs.
89.             LaunchCompleted(e);
90.         }
91. 
92.         private Frame CreateRootFrame()
93.         {
94.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
95.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
96. 
97.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
98.             // just ensure that the window is active
99.             if (rootFrame == null)
100.             {
101.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
102.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
103. 
104.                 // Set the default language
105.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
106.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
107. 
108.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
109.                 // TODO[migrate-005]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
110.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
111.             }
112. 
113.             return rootFrame;
114.         }

43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)

46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();

48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)

51.             }

59.             else

61.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
62.             }

65.         /// <summary>
66.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
67.         /// sample scenario page.
68.         /// </summary>
69.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
70.         /// <param name="e"></param>
71.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
72.         {

74.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
75.

77.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
78.             if (s != null)

94.         /// <summary>
95.         /// Display a message to the user.
96.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
97.         /// </summary>
98.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
99.         /// <param name="type"></param>
100.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
101.         {
102.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
103.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
104.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
105.             {
106.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
107.             }
108.             else
109.             {
110.                 // TODO[migrate-021]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
111.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
112.             }
113.         }
114. 
115.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
116.         {
117.             switch (type)
118.             {
119.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
121.                     break;
122.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
123.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
124.                     break;
125.             }
126. 
127.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
128. 
129.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
130.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
131.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
132.             {
133.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
134.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
135.             }
136.             else
137.             {
138.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
139.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
140.             }
141. 
142. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
143. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
144. 			if (peer != null)
145. 			{
146. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
147. 			}
148. 		}

152.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
153.         }

158.         }

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Threading.Tasks;
15. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
16. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
17. using Windows.Storage;
18. 
19. namespace SDKTemplate
20. {
21.     public partial class MainPage : Page
22.     {
23.         public const string FEATURE_NAME = "Background tasks";
24. 
25.         List<Scenario> scenarios = new List<Scenario>
26.         {
27.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task", ClassType=typeof(SampleBackgroundTask)},
28.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task with Condition", ClassType=typeof(SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition)},
29.             new Scenario() { Title="Servicing Complete Task", ClassType=typeof(ServicingCompleteTask)},
30.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task with Time Trigger", ClassType=typeof(TimeTriggeredTask) },
31.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task with Application Trigger", ClassType=typeof(ApplicationTriggerTask) },
32.             new Scenario() { Title="Grouped Background Task", ClassType=typeof(GroupedBackgroundTask) },
33.         };
34.     }

36.     public class Scenario
37.     {
38.         public string Title { get; set; }
39.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
40.     }

42. 
43. namespace SDKTemplate
44. {
45.     class BackgroundTaskSample
46.     {
47.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskEntryPoint = "Tasks.SampleBackgroundTask";
48.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskName = "SampleBackgroundTask";
49.         public static string SampleBackgroundTaskProgress = "";
50.         public static bool SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = false;
51. 
52.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName = "SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition";
53.         public static string SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionProgress = "";
54.         public static bool SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = false;
55. 
56.         public const string ServicingCompleteTaskEntryPoint = "Tasks.ServicingComplete";
57.         public const string ServicingCompleteTaskName = "ServicingCompleteTask";
58.         public static string ServicingCompleteTaskProgress = "";
59.         public static bool ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = false;
60. 
61.         public const string TimeTriggeredTaskName = "TimeTriggeredTask";
62.         public static string TimeTriggeredTaskProgress = "";
63.         public static bool TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = false;
64. 
65.         public const string ApplicationTriggerTaskName = "ApplicationTriggerTask";
66.         public static string ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress = "";
67.         public static string ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "";
68.         public static bool ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = false;
69. 
70.         public const string GroupedBackgroundTaskName = "GroupedBackgroundTask";
71.         public const string BackgroundTaskGroupId = "3F2504E0-5F89-41D3-9A0C-0405E82C3333";
72.         public const string BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName = "Background Task Group";
73.         public static string GroupedBackgroundTaskProgress = "";
74.         public static bool GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = false;
75. 
76.         /// <summary>
77.         /// Register a background task with the specified taskEntryPoint, name, trigger,
78.         /// and condition (optional).
79.         /// </summary>
80.         /// <param name="taskEntryPoint">Task entry point for the background task.</param>
81.         /// <param name="name">A name for the background task.</param>
82.         /// <param name="trigger">The trigger for the background task.</param>
83.         /// <param name="condition">An optional conditional event that must be true for the task to fire.</param>
84.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistration RegisterBackgroundTask(String taskEntryPoint, String name, IBackgroundTrigger trigger, IBackgroundCondition condition, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
85.         {
86.             if (TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(name))
87.             {
88.                 // If the user denies access, the task will not run.
89.                 var requestTask = BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync();
90.             }
91. 
92.             // TODO[migrate-022]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
93.             var builder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
94. 
95.             builder.Name = name;
96.             builder.TaskEntryPoint = taskEntryPoint;
97.             builder.SetTrigger(trigger);
98. 
99.             if (condition != null)
100.             {
101.                 builder.AddCondition(condition);
102. 
103.                 //
104.                 // If the condition changes while the background task is executing then it will
105.                 // be canceled.
106.                 //
107.                 builder.CancelOnConditionLoss = true;
108.             }
109. 
110.             if (group != null)
111.             {
112.                 builder.TaskGroup = group;
113.             }
114. 
115.             BackgroundTaskRegistration task = builder.Register();
116. 
117.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, true);
118. 
119.             //
120.             // Remove previous completion status.
121.             //
122.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
123.             settings.Values.Remove(name);
124. 
125.             return task;
126.         }
127. 
128.         /// <summary>
129.         /// Unregister background tasks with specified name.
130.         /// </summary>
131.         /// <param name="name">Name of the background task to unregister.</param>
132.         public static void UnregisterBackgroundTasks(String name, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
133.         {
134.             //
135.             // If the given task group is registered then loop through all background tasks associated with it
136.             // and unregister any with the given name.
137.             //
138.             if (group != null)
139.             {
140.                 foreach (var cur in group.AllTasks)
141.                 {
142.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
143.                     {
144.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
145.                     }
146.                 }
147.             }
148.             else
149.             {
150.                 //
151.                 // Loop through all ungrouped background tasks and unregister any with the given name.
152.                 //
153.                 foreach (var cur in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
154.                 {
155.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
156.                     {
157.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
158.                     }
159.                 }
160.             }
161. 
162.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, false);
163.         }
164. 
165.         /// <summary>
166.         /// Retrieve a registered background task group. If no group is registered with the given id,
167.         /// then create a new one and return it.
168.         /// </summary>
169.         /// <returns>The task group associated with the given id</returns>
170.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup GetTaskGroup(string id, string groupName)
171.         {
172.             var group = BackgroundTaskRegistration.GetTaskGroup(id);
173. 
174.             if (group == null)
175.             {
176.                 group = new BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup(id, groupName);
177.             }
178. 
179.             return group;
180.         }
181. 
182.         /// <summary>
183.         /// Store the registration status of a background task with a given name.
184.         /// </summary>
185.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to store registration status for.</param>
186.         /// <param name="registered">TRUE if registered, FALSE if unregistered.</param>
187.         public static void UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(String name, bool registered)
188.         {
189.             switch (name)
190.             {
191.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
192.                     SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
193.                     break;
194.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
195.                     SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = registered;
196.                     break;
197.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
198.                     ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = registered;
199.                     break;
200.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
201.                     TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = registered;
202.                     break;
203.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
204.                     ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = registered;
205.                     break;
206.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
207.                     GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
208.                     break;
209.             }
210.         }
211. 
212.         /// <summary>
213.         /// Get the registration / completion status of the background task with
214.         /// given name.
215.         /// </summary>
216.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to retreive registration status.</param>
217.         public static String GetBackgroundTaskStatus(String name)
218.         {
219.             var registered = false;
220.             switch (name)
221.             {
222.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
223.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
224.                     break;
225.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
226.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered;
227.                     break;
228.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
229.                     registered = ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered;
230.                     break;
231.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
232.                     registered = TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered;
233.                     break;
234.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
235.                     registered = ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
236.                     break;
237.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
238.                     registered = GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered;
239.                     break;
240.             }
241. 
242.             var status = registered ? "Registered" : "Unregistered";
243. 
244.             object taskStatus;
245.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
246.             if (settings.Values.TryGetValue(name, out taskStatus))
247.             {
248.                 status += " - " + taskStatus.ToString();
249.             }
250. 
251.             return status;
252.         }
253. 
254.         /// <summary>
255.         /// Determine if task with given name requires background access.
256.         /// </summary>
257.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to query background access requirement.</param>
258.         public static bool TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(String name)
259.         {
260.             if ((name == TimeTriggeredTaskName) ||
261.                 (name == ApplicationTriggerTaskName))
262.             {
263.                 return true;
264.             }
265.             else
266.             {
267.                 return false;
268.             }
269.         }
270.     }
271. }

12. using System;
13. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
14. using Windows.Storage;
15. using Windows.UI.Core;
16. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
17. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
19. using SDKTemplate;
20. 
21. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=234238
22. 
23. namespace SDKTemplate
24. {
25.     /// <summary>
26.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
27.     /// </summary>
28.     public sealed partial class SampleBackgroundTask : Page
29.     {
30.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
31.         // as NotifyUser()
32.         private MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
33. 
34.         public SampleBackgroundTask()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.         }
38. 
39.         /// <summary>
40.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
41.         /// </summary>
42.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
43.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
44.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             foreach (var task in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
47.             {
48.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName)
49.                 {
50.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);
51.                     BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName, true);
52.                     break;
53.                 }
54.             }
55. 
56.             UpdateUI();
57.         }
58. 
59.         /// <summary>
60.         /// Register a SampleBackgroundTask.
61.         /// </summary>
62.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
63.         /// <param name="e"></param>
64.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
65.         {
66.             var task = BackgroundTaskSample.RegisterBackgroundTask(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskEntryPoint,
67.                                                                    BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName,
68.                                                                    new SystemTrigger(SystemTriggerType.TimeZoneChange, false),
69.                                                                    null);
70.             AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task);
71.             UpdateUI();
72.         }
73. 
74.         /// <summary>
75.         /// Unregister a SampleBackgroundTask.
76.         /// </summary>
77.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
78.         /// <param name="e"></param>
79.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
80.         {
81.             BackgroundTaskSample.UnregisterBackgroundTasks(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName);
82.             UpdateUI();
83.         }
84. 
85.         /// <summary>
86.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
87.         /// </summary>
88.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
89.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
90.         {
91.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
92.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
93.         }
94. 
95.         /// <summary>
96.         /// Handle background task progress.
97.         /// </summary>
98.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting progress.</param>
99.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the progress report.</param>
100.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
101.         {
102.             // TODO[migrate-023]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
103.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
104.             {
105.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
106.                 BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskProgress = progress;
107.                 UpdateUI();
108.             });
109.         }
110. 
111.         /// <summary>
112.         /// Handle background task completion.
113.         /// </summary>
114.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting completion.</param>
115.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the completion report.</param>
116.         private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs args)
117.         {
118.             UpdateUI();
119.         }
120. 
121.         /// <summary>
122.         /// Update the scenario UI.
123.         /// </summary>
124.         private async void UpdateUI()
125.         {
126.             // TODO[migrate-024]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
127.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
128.             () =>
129.             {
130.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
131.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
132.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskProgress;
133.                 Status.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.GetBackgroundTaskStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName);
134.             });
135.         }
136.     }
137. }
138.

26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition : Page
30.     {
31.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
32.         // as NotifyUser()
33.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
34. 
35.         public SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition()
36.         {
37.             this.InitializeComponent();
38.         }
39. 
40.         /// <summary>
41.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
42.         /// </summary>
43.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
44.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
45.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
46.         {
47.             foreach (var task in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
48.             {
49.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName)
50.                 {
51.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);
52.                     BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName, true);
53.                     break;
54.                 }
55.             }
56. 
57.             UpdateUI();
58.         }
59. 
60.         /// <summary>
61.         /// Register a SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition.
62.         /// </summary>
63.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
64.         /// <param name="e"></param>
65.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
66.         {
67.             var task = BackgroundTaskSample.RegisterBackgroundTask(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskEntryPoint,
68.                                                                    BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName,
69.                                                                    new SystemTrigger(SystemTriggerType.TimeZoneChange, false),
70.                                                                    new SystemCondition(SystemConditionType.InternetAvailable));
71.             AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task);
72.             UpdateUI();
73.         }
74. 
75.         /// <summary>
76.         /// Unregister a SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition.
77.         /// </summary>
78.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
79.         /// <param name="e"></param>
80.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
81.         {
82.             BackgroundTaskSample.UnregisterBackgroundTasks(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName);
83.             UpdateUI();
84.         }
85. 
86.         /// <summary>
87.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
88.         /// </summary>
89.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
90.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
91.         {
92.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
93.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
94.         }
95. 
96.         /// <summary>
97.         /// Handle background task progress.
98.         /// </summary>
99.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting progress.</param>
100.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the progress report.</param>
101.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
102.         {
103.             // TODO[migrate-025]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
104.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
105.             {
106.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
107.                 BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionProgress = progress;
108.                 UpdateUI();
109.             });
110.         }
111. 
112.         /// <summary>
113.         /// Handle background task completion.
114.         /// </summary>
115.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting completion.</param>
116.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the completion report.</param>
117.         private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs args)
118.         {
119.             UpdateUI();
120.         }
121. 
122.         /// <summary>
123.         /// Update the scenario UI.
124.         /// </summary>
125.         private async void UpdateUI()
126.         {
127.             // TODO[migrate-026]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
128.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
129.             () =>
130.             {
131.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered;
132.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered;
133.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionProgress;
134.                 Status.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.GetBackgroundTaskStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName);
135.             });
136.         }
137.     }
138. }

25.     /// <summary>
26.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
27.     /// </summary>
28.     public sealed partial class ApplicationTriggerTask : Page
29.     {
30.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
31.         // as NotifyUser()
32.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
33. 
34.         // A pointer to the ApplicationTrigger so we can signal it later
35.         ApplicationTrigger trigger = null;
36. 
37.         public ApplicationTriggerTask()
38.         {
39.             this.InitializeComponent();
40.         }
41. 
42.         /// <summary>
43.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
44.         /// </summary>
45.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
46.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
47.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
48.         {
49.             foreach (var task in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
50.             {
51.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName)
52.                 {
53.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);
54.                     BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName, true);
55.                     break;
56.                 }
57.             }
58. 
59.             trigger = new ApplicationTrigger();
60.             UpdateUI();
61.         }
62. 
63.         /// <summary>
64.         /// Register a ApplicationTriggerTask.
65.         /// </summary>
66.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
67.         /// <param name="e"></param>
68.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
69.         {
70.             var task = BackgroundTaskSample.RegisterBackgroundTask(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskEntryPoint,
71.                                                                    BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName,
72.                                                                    trigger,
73.                                                                    null);
74.             AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task);
75.             UpdateUI();
76.         }
77. 
78.         /// <summary>
79.         /// Unregister a ApplicationTriggerTask.
80.         /// </summary>
81.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
82.         /// <param name="e"></param>
83.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
84.         {
85.             BackgroundTaskSample.UnregisterBackgroundTasks(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName);
86.             BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "";
87.             UpdateUI();
88.         }
89. 
90.         /// <summary>
91.         /// Signal a ApplicationTriggerTask.
92.         /// </summary>
93.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
94.         /// <param name="e"></param>
95.         private async void SignalBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
96.         {
97.             // Reset the completion status
98.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
99.             settings.Values.Remove(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName);
100. 
101.             //Signal the ApplicationTrigger
102.             var result = await trigger.RequestAsync();
103.             BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "Signal result: " + result.ToString();
104.             UpdateUI();
105.         }
106. 
107.         /// <summary>
108.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
109.         /// </summary>
110.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
111.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
112.         {
113.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
114.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
115.         }
116. 
117.         /// <summary>
118.         /// Handle background task progress.
119.         /// </summary>
120.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting progress.</param>
121.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the progress report.</param>
122.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
123.         {
124.             // TODO[migrate-031]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
125.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
126.             {
127.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
128.                 BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress = progress;
129.                 UpdateUI();
130.             });
131.         }
132. 
133.         /// <summary>
134.         /// Handle background task completion.
135.         /// </summary>
136.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting completion.</param>
137.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the completion report.</param>
138.         private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs args)
139.         {
140.             UpdateUI();
141.         }
142. 
143.         /// <summary>
144.         /// Update the scenario UI.
145.         /// </summary>
146.         private async void UpdateUI()
147.         {
148.             // TODO[migrate-032]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
149.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
150.             () =>
151.             {
152.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
153.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
154.                 SignalButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered & (trigger != null);
155.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress;
156.                 Result.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskResult;
157.                 Status.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.GetBackgroundTaskStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName);
158.             });
159.         }

31.         // as NotifyUser()

37.         }

47.         {
48.             group = BackgroundTaskSample.GetTaskGroup(BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupId, BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName);
49. 
50.             foreach (var task in group.AllTasks)
51.             {
52.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.GroupedBackgroundTaskName)
53.                 {
54.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);

95.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
96.         /// <param name="e"></param>
97.         private void UnregisterUngroupedTasks(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
98.         {
99.             foreach(var cur in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
100.             {
101.                 cur.Value.Unregister(true);
102.                 BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(cur.Value.Name, false);
103.             }
104.         }

83.         }
84. 
85.         /// <summary>
86.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
87.         /// </summary>
88.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
89.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
90.         {
91.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
92.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
93.         }
94. 
95.         /// <summary>
96.         /// Handle background task progress.
97.         /// </summary>
98.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting progress.</param>
99.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the progress report.</param>
100.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
101.         {
102.             // TODO[migrate-027]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
103.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
104.             {
105.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
106.                 BackgroundTaskSample.ServicingCompleteTaskProgress = progress;
107.                 UpdateUI();
108.             });
109.         }
110. 
111.         /// <summary>
112.         /// Handle background task completion.
113.         /// </summary>
114.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting completion.</param>
115.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the completion report.</param>
116.         private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs args)
117.         {
118.             UpdateUI();
119.         }
120. 
121.         /// <summary>
122.         /// Update the scenario UI.
123.         /// </summary>
124.         private async void UpdateUI()
125.         {
126.             // TODO[migrate-028]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
127.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
128.             () =>
129.             {
130.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered;
131.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered;
132.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.ServicingCompleteTaskProgress;

11. using System.Diagnostics;
12. using System.Threading;

16. //
17. // The namespace for the background tasks.
18. //
19. namespace Tasks
20. {
21.     //
22.     // A background task always implements the IBackgroundTask interface.
23.     //
24.     // TODO[migrate-036]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
25.     public sealed class ServicingComplete : IBackgroundTask
26.     {
27.         volatile bool _cancelRequested = false;
28. 
29.         //
30.         // The Run method is the entry point of a background task.
31.         //
32.         public void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
33.         {
34.             Debug.WriteLine("ServicingComplete " + taskInstance.Task.Name + " starting...");
35. 
36.             //
37.             // Associate a cancellation handler with the background task.
38.             //
39.             taskInstance.Canceled += new BackgroundTaskCanceledEventHandler(OnCanceled);
40. 
41.             //
42.             // Do background task activity for servicing complete.
43.             //
44.             uint Progress;
45.             for (Progress = 0; Progress <= 100; Progress += 10)
46.             {
47.                 //
48.                 // If the cancellation handler indicated that the task was canceled, stop doing the task.
49.                 //
50.                 if (_cancelRequested)
51.                 {
52.                     break;
53.                 }
54. 
55.                 //
56.                 // Indicate progress to foreground application.
57.                 //
58.                 taskInstance.Progress = Progress;
59.             }
60. 
61.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
62.             var key = taskInstance.Task.Name;
63. 
64.             //
65.             // Write to LocalSettings to indicate that this background task ran.
66.             //
67.             settings.Values[key] = (Progress < 100) ? "Canceled" : "Completed";
68.             Debug.WriteLine("ServicingComplete " + taskInstance.Task.Name + ((Progress < 100) ? " Canceled" : " Completed"));
69.         }
70. 
71.         //
72.         // Handles background task cancellation.
73.         //
74.         private void OnCanceled(IBackgroundTaskInstance sender, BackgroundTaskCancellationReason reason)
75.         {
76.             //
77.             // Indicate that the background task is canceled.
78.             //
79.             _cancelRequested = true;
80. 
81.             Debug.WriteLine("ServicingComplete " + sender.Task.Name + " Cancel Requested...");
82.         }
83.     }
84. }

11. using System;
12. using System.Diagnostics;
13. using System.Threading;
14. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
15. using Windows.Foundation;
16. using Windows.Storage;
17. using Windows.System.Threading;
18. 
19. //
20. // The namespace for the background tasks.
21. //
22. namespace Tasks
23. {
24.     //
25.     // A background task always implements the IBackgroundTask interface.
26.     //
27.     // TODO[migrate-035]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
28.     public sealed class SampleBackgroundTask : IBackgroundTask
29.     {
30.         BackgroundTaskCancellationReason _cancelReason = BackgroundTaskCancellationReason.Abort;
31.         volatile bool _cancelRequested = false;
32.         BackgroundTaskDeferral _deferral = null;
33.         ThreadPoolTimer _periodicTimer = null;
34.         uint _progress = 0;
35.         IBackgroundTaskInstance _taskInstance = null;
36. 
37.         //
38.         // The Run method is the entry point of a background task.
39.         //
40.         public void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
41.         {
42.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + taskInstance.Task.Name + " Starting...");
43. 
44.             //
45.             // Query BackgroundWorkCost
46.             // Guidance: If BackgroundWorkCost is high, then perform only the minimum amount
47.             // of work in the background task and return immediately.
48.             //
49.             var cost = BackgroundWorkCost.CurrentBackgroundWorkCost;
50.             var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
51.             settings.Values["BackgroundWorkCost"] = cost.ToString();
52. 
53.             //
54.             // Associate a cancellation handler with the background task.
55.             //
56.             taskInstance.Canceled += new BackgroundTaskCanceledEventHandler(OnCanceled);
57. 
58.             //
59.             // Get the deferral object from the task instance, and take a reference to the taskInstance;
60.             //
61.             _deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
62.             _taskInstance = taskInstance;
63. 
64.             _periodicTimer = ThreadPoolTimer.CreatePeriodicTimer(new TimerElapsedHandler(PeriodicTimerCallback), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
65.         }
66. 
67.         //
68.         // Handles background task cancellation.
69.         //
70.         private void OnCanceled(IBackgroundTaskInstance sender, BackgroundTaskCancellationReason reason)
71.         {
72.             //
73.             // Indicate that the background task is canceled.
74.             //
75.             _cancelRequested = true;
76.             _cancelReason = reason;
77. 
78.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + sender.Task.Name + " Cancel Requested...");
79.         }
80. 
81.         //
82.         // Simulate the background task activity.
83.         //
84.         private void PeriodicTimerCallback(ThreadPoolTimer timer)
85.         {
86.             if ((_cancelRequested == false) && (_progress < 100))
87.             {
88.                 _progress += 10;
89.                 _taskInstance.Progress = _progress;
90.             }
91.             else
92.             {
93.                 _periodicTimer.Cancel();
94. 
95.                 var key = _taskInstance.Task.Name;
96. 
97.                 //
98.                 // Record that this background task ran.
99.                 //
100.                 String taskStatus = (_progress < 100) ? "Canceled with reason: " + _cancelReason.ToString() : "Completed";
101.                 var settings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
102.                 settings.Values[key] = taskStatus;
103.                 Debug.WriteLine("Background " + _taskInstance.Task.Name + settings.Values[key]);
104. 
105.                 //
106.                 // Indicate that the background task has completed.
107.                 //
108.                 _deferral.Complete();
109.             }
110.         }
111.     }
112. }

<a id="windowing"></a>
## Windowing: Window.Current / ApplicationView / CoreWindow → AppWindow`Window.Current`, `ApplicationView`, and `CoreWindow` are gone. Track the main window yourself and use `Microsoft.UI.Windowing.AppWindow`. See the official [windowing guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/windowing).UWP (replace this):```csharp
var win = Window.Current;
ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().TryResizeView(new Size(800, 600));
```WinUI 3 — expose `MainWindow` from `App`:```csharp
public partial class App : Application
{
    // With <Nullable>enable</Nullable>, declare as nullable since the assignment only happens during OnLaunched.
    public static Window? MainWindow { get; private set; }    protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
    {
        var window = new MainWindow();
        MainWindow = window;
        window.Activate();
    }
}
```Consumers running after `OnLaunched` can safely null-forgive:```csharp
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow!);
```Resize via `AppWindow`:```csharp
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);
var windowId = Microsoft.UI.Win32Interop.GetWindowIdFromWindow(hwnd);
var appWindow = AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(windowId);
appWindow.Resize(new SizeInt32(800, 600));
```### Initialization order — keep MainWindow's constructor inert`App.MainWindow` (or any equivalent static window reference) is `null` for the entire duration of `new MainWindow()` — the right-hand side runs to completion BEFORE the assignment happens. Anything inside that constructor that reads the static — directly or transitively — sees `null` and throws `E_POINTER` (0x80004003) at startup. The build is clean; the analyzer is silent; only a runtime launch reveals it.APIs that commonly trip this race when called from a `Page` reached during `MainWindow` construction:- `WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow)`
- `XamlRoot` / `Content.XamlRoot` access through a static-window helper
- `PrintManagerInterop.GetForWindow(hwnd)` / `ShowPrintUIForWindowAsync(hwnd)`
- `FileOpenPicker` / `FileSavePicker` / `FolderPicker` (require an HWND)
- `ContentDialog.ShowAsync()` when the dialog's `XamlRoot` is sourced from a static
- `AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(...)` chained through `App.MainWindow`
- Any custom title bar / `SetTitleBar` helper that resolves the HWND staticallySafe pattern — assign before activate, defer first navigation:```csharp
// App.xaml.cs
protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
{
    var window = new MainWindow();
    MainWindow = window;
    window.Activate();
}
```

The `MainWindow` constructor must do only `InitializeComponent` and inert setup — no navigation to a `Page` that reads `
`App.MainWindow`. Defer the first navigation until after `Activate` completes, either from a `Loaded` handler hooked in `
`InitializeComponent` or by exposing a `NavigateToInitialPage()` method and calling it from `App.OnLaunched` after the st
tatic is assigned.

```csharp
// MainWindow.xaml.cs
public MainWindow()
{
    InitializeComponent();
    this.Activated += (_, _) =>
    {
        if (!_navigated)
        {
            _navigated = true;
            RootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage));
        }
    };
}
private bool _navigated;
```

Validator catches this race with a 10s smoke launch after the build healthcheck passes — see `Validate-UwpMigration.ps1`
` Section 7.

### AppWindow API replacements

| UWP API | WinUI 3 API |
|---------|-------------|
| `ApplicationView.TryResizeView` | `AppWindow.Resize` |
| `AppWindow.TryCreateAsync` | `AppWindow.Create` |
| `AppWindow.TryShowAsync` | `AppWindow.Show` |
| `AppWindow.TryConsolidateAsync` | `AppWindow.Destroy` |
| `AppWindow.RequestMoveXxx` | `AppWindow.Move` |
| `AppWindow.RequestPresentation` | `AppWindow.SetPresenter` |
| `CoreApplicationViewTitleBar` | `AppWindowTitleBar` |
| `CoreApplicationView.TitleBar.ExtendViewIntoTitleBar` | `AppWindow.TitleBar.ExtendsContentIntoTitleBar` |
